STATEMENT Research.DerivativeSwitch.OrthogonalComparison.inverse_preserves_global_homotopy: {ι : Type u_1} →
  {V : Type u} →
    [inst : CategoryTheory.Category.{v, u} V] →
      [inst_1 : @CategoryTheory.Preadditive.{v, u} V inst] →
        {c : ComplexShape.{u_1} ι} →
          {C D G :
              @HomologicalComplex.{v, u, u_1} ι V inst
                (@CategoryTheory.Preadditive.preadditiveHasZeroMorphisms.{v, u} V inst inst_1) c} →
            (j :

                  (@HomologicalComplex.{v, u, u_1} ι V inst
                    (@CategoryTheory.Preadditive.preadditiveHasZeroMorphisms.{v, u} V inst inst_1) c)
                  (@CategoryTheory.CategoryStruct.toQuiver.{max u_1 v, max (max u u_1) v}
                    (@HomologicalComplex.{v, u, u_1} ι V inst
                      (@CategoryTheory.Preadditive.preadditiveHasZeroMorphisms.{v, u} V inst inst_1) c)
                    (@CategoryTheory.Category.toCategoryStruct.{max u_1 v, max (max u u_1) v}
                      (@HomologicalComplex.{v, u, u_1} ι V inst
                        (@CategoryTheory.Preadditive.preadditiveHasZeroMorphisms.{v, u} V inst inst_1) c)
                      (@HomologicalComplex.instCategory.{v, u, u_1} ι V inst
                        (@CategoryTheory.Preadditive.preadditiveHasZeroMorphisms.{v, u} V inst inst_1) c)))
                  C D) →
              (k :
                  @Quiver.Hom.{max u_1 v, max (max u u_1) v}
                    (@HomologicalComplex.{v, u, u_1} ι V inst
                      (@CategoryTheory.Preadditive.preadditiveHasZeroMorphisms.{v, u} V inst inst_1) c)
                    (@CategoryTheory.CategoryStruct.toQuiver.{max u_1 v, max (max u u_1) v}
                      (@HomologicalComplex.{v, u, u_1} ι V inst
                        (@CategoryTheory.Preadditive.preadditiveHasZeroMorphisms.{v, u} V inst inst_1) c)
                      (@CategoryTheory.Category.toCategoryStruct.{max u_1 v, max (max u u_1) v}
                        (@HomologicalComplex.{v, u, u_1} ι V inst
                          (@CategoryTheory.Preadditive.preadditiveHasZeroMorphisms.{v, u} V inst inst_1) c)
                        (@HomologicalComplex.instCategory.{v, u, u_1} ι V inst
                          (@CategoryTheory.Preadditive.preadditiveHasZeroMorphisms.{v, u} V inst inst_1) c)))
                    D C) →
                (p :
                    @Quiver.Hom.{max u_1 v, max (max u u_1) v}
                      (@HomologicalComplex.{v, u, u_1} ι V inst
